Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any linear_algebra module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 117. The projective resolution has length at most 749 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to linear_algebra theory, we decompose the problem into 117 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 749. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
